Notwithstanding sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.415#stat.97A.415.1">97A.415, subdivision 1</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97B.301#stat.97B.301.2">97B.301, subdivision 2</a>, the holder of the license may purchase additional licenses or permits for taking deer and may take additional deer under those licenses or permits, provided the holder adheres to the bag limits established for that permit area.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) A person who obtains a license under paragraph (a) must allow public deer hunting on their land during that deer-hunting season, with the exception of the first Saturday and Sunday during the deer-hunting season applicable to the license issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.475#stat.97A.475.2">97A.475, subdivision 2</a>, clause (5).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.441.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Duplicate licenses.</span></h2>
          <p>There is no fee for a duplicate license if the original license was issued without a fee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.441.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Angling assistance to disabled residents.</span></h2>
          <p>An individual who is providing angling assistance to a disabled resident licensed under subdivision 1, 4, or 5, is not required to possess a license to take fish by angling provided that no lines in addition to those permitted for an individual under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97C.315">97C.315</a> are in the water.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.441.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Taking wild animals to prevent and control wildlife disease.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may issue, without a fee, licenses to take wild animals for the purposes of wildlife disease prevention and control.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.441.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.61.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 61</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/384/#laws.1.7.0">1987 c 384 art 1 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/172/#laws.0.55.0">1993 c 172 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/231/#laws.0.26.0">1993 c 231 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/623/#laws.1.23.0">1994 c 623 art 1 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/632/#laws.2.24.0">1994 c 632 art 2 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/95/#laws.0.1.0">1997 c 95 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/473/#laws.0.3.0">2000 c 473 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/185/#laws.0.28.0">2001 c 185 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/128/#laws.1.56.0">2003 c 128 art 1 s 56</a>,57; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/56/#laws.0.1.0">2005 c 56 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/146/#laws.0.14.0">2005 c 146 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/131/#laws.1.26.0">2007 c 131 art 1 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/368/#laws.2.24.0">2008 c 368 art 2 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/176/#laws.2.20.0">2009 c 176 art 2 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/277/#laws.1.35.0">2012 c 277 art 1 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/121/#laws.0.30.0">2013 c 121 s 30</a>,31; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/290/#laws.0.23.0">2014 c 290 s 23</a>-26; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/93/#laws.2.79.0">2017 c 93 art 2 s 79</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.445">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.445 EXEMPTIONS FROM LICENSE REQUIREMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.445.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Angling; Take a Kid Fishing Weekends.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A resident age 16 years or older may take fish by angling without an angling license and may take fish by spearing from a dark house without a spearing license and without a fish house or dark house license during one three-day consecutive period of the open-water angling season and one three-day consecutive period of the ice angling season designated by the commissioner if the resident is accompanied by a child who is under age 16. The commissioner may, by written order published in the State Register, establish the three-day consecutive periods. The written order is not subject to the rulemaking provisions of chapter 14 and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.386">14.386</a> does not apply.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The commissioner may designate and publicize the three-day periods as "Take a Kid Fishing Weekend" for the open-water angling season and "Take a Kid Ice Fishing Weekend" for the ice angling season. The commissioner shall announce the date of each three-day weekend at least 30 days in advance of the date it occurs.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.445.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Angling in state parks.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A resident may take fish by angling without an angling license:</p>
          <p>(1) when shore fishing or wading on state-owned land within a state park; or</p>
          <p>(2) when angling from a boat or float or through the ice on water bodies completely encompassed within the statutory boundary of the state park.</p>
          <p>(b) The exemption from an angling license does not apply to waters where a trout stamp is required.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.445.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Angling; institutional residents.</span></h2>
          <p>A license is not required to take fish by angling with the written consent of the superintendent or chief executive of the institution for the following persons:</p>
          <p>(1) a resident of a state hospital;</p>
          <p>(2) a patient of a United States Veterans Administration hospital;</p>
          <p>(3) an inmate of a state correctional facility;</p>
          <p>(4) a resident of a licensed nursing or boarding care home, a person who is enrolled in and regularly participates in an adult day care program or other similar organized activity sponsored by a licensed nursing or boarding care home, or a resident of a licensed board and lodging facility; and</p>
          <p>(5) a resident of a drug or alcohol residential treatment program under the age of 20.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.445.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/290/#laws.0.70.0">2014 c 290 s 70</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.445.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Angling; Take a Mom Fishing Weekend.</span></h2>
          <p>Any mother who is a resident of Minnesota may take fish by angling without a license during the Saturday and Sunday of the angling season that coincides with Mother's Day. The commissioner shall publicize the Saturday and Sunday as "Take a Mom Fishing Weekend."</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.445.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Small-game hunting; Take a Kid Hunting Weekend.</span></h2>
          <p>A resident over age 18 may take small game by hunting without a license during one Saturday and Sunday of the small-game-hunting season designated by rule of the commissioner if accompanied by a child who is under age 16. The commissioner shall publicize the Saturday and Sunday as "Take a Kid Hunting Weekend."</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.445.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">License system.</span></h2>
          <p>In the event of a disruption in the availability of hunting and angling licenses, the commissioner may publish in the State Register a notice that exempts residents and nonresidents from requirements to possess a license to take game or fish.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.445.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.62.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 62</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/149/#laws.1.29.0">1987 c 149 art 1 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/384/#laws.1.8.0">1987 c 384 art 1 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/404/#laws.0.120.0">1987 c 404 s 120</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/588/#laws.0.5.0">1988 c 588 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/242/#laws.0.1.0">1989 c 242 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/605/#laws.0.4.0">1990 c 605 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/241/#laws.0.5.0">1991 c 241 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/259/#laws.0.23.0">1991 c 259 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/473/#laws.0.4.0">2000 c 473 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/212/#laws.1.4.0">2006 c 212 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/131/#laws.1.27.0">2007 c 131 art 1 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/176/#laws.2.21.0">2009 c 176 art 2 s 21</a>,22; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/2/#laws.5.31.0">1Sp2011 c 2 art 5 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/121/#laws.0.32.0">2013 c 121 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/90/#laws.1.28.0">2024 c 90 art 1 s 28</a></p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>Subdivision 6, as added by Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, section 28, is effective upon full implementation of the replacement electronic license system. The commissioner of natural resources must notify the revisor of statutes when the replacement electronic license system is fully implemented. Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, section 52.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.451">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.451 LICENSE REQUIREMENTS AND EXEMPTIONS RELATING TO AGE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.451.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/437/#laws.0.6.0">1988 c 437 s 6</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.451.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Residents under age 16; fishing.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A resident under the age of 16 years may take fish without a license.</p>
          <p>(b) A resident under the age of 16 may net ciscoes and whitefish for personal consumption without the license required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.475#stat.97A.475.13">97A.475, subdivision 13</a>. A resident netting ciscoes and whitefish under this paragraph must follow all other applicable requirements for netting ciscoes and whitefish for personal consumption.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.451.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Residents age 16 or 17; spearing.</span></h2>
          <p>Residents age 16 or over and under age 18 may take fish by spearing without a spearing license but must possess a fishing license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.475#stat.97A.475.6">97A.475, subdivision 6</a>, clause (7).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.451.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Residents and nonresidents under age 16; small game.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A resident or nonresident under age 16 may not obtain a small-game license but may take small game by firearms or bow and arrow without a license if the resident or nonresident is:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) age 14 or 15 and possesses a firearms safety certificate;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) age 13, possesses a firearms safety certificate, and is accompanied by a parent or guardian;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) age 13, 14, or 15, and possesses an apprentice-hunter validation as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97B.022">97B.022</a>; or</p>
          <p class="in">(4) age 12 or under and is accompanied by a parent or guardian.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) A resident under age 16 may take small game, other than wolves, by trapping without a small-game license, but a resident 13 years of age or older must have a trapping license. A resident under age 13 may trap small game, other than wolves, without a trapping license, but may not register fisher, otter, bobcat, or pine marten unless the resident is at least age five. Any fisher, otter, bobcat, or pine marten taken by a resident under age five must be included in the limit of the accompanying parent or guardian.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) A resident or nonresident under age 13 must obtain a free turkey license to take turkey and may take a turkey without a firearms safety certificate if the resident or nonresident is accompanied by an adult parent or guardian who has a firearms safety certificate.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) A resident under age 13 may apply for a prairie-chicken license and may take a prairie chicken without a firearms safety certificate if the resident is accompanied by an adult parent or guardian who has a firearms safety certificate.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.451.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/277/#laws.2.37.0">2012 c 277 art 2 s 37</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.451.3b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3b.<span class="headnote">Nonresidents age 16 or over and under age 18; small game.</span></h2>
          <p> A nonresident age 16 or over and under age 18 may take small game by firearms or archery and may obtain a small-game license at the youth fee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.475#stat.97A.475.3">97A.475, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (a), clause (14), if the nonresident possesses a firearms safety certificate or an apprentice-hunter validation as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97B.022">97B.022</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.451.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Residents and nonresidents under age 16; big game.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A resident or nonresident age 12, 13, 14, or 15 may not obtain a license to take big game unless the person possesses a firearms safety certificate or an apprentice-hunter validation as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97B.022">97B.022</a>. A nonresident age 12 or 13 must be accompanied by a parent or guardian to hunt big game.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) A resident or nonresident age ten or 11 must obtain a license under paragraph (c) and may take big game, provided the person is under the direct supervision of a parent or guardian where the parent or guardian is within immediate reach.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) A resident or nonresident age ten, 11, or 12 must obtain a license to take big game under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.475#stat.97A.475.2">97A.475, subdivision 2</a> or 3.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.451.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/121/#laws.0.59.0">2013 c 121 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.451.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Nonresident youth; angling.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A nonresident under age 16 may:</p>
          <p>(1) take fish by angling without a license if a parent or guardian has a fishing license. Fish taken by a nonresident under age 16 without a license must be included in the limit of the parent or guardian;</p>
          <p>(2) purchase a youth fishing license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.475#stat.97A.475.7">97A.475, subdivision 7</a>, paragraph (a), clause (8), and possess a limit of fish; or</p>
          <p>(3) be included under a nonresident family angling license and possess a limit of fish.</p>
          <p>(b) A nonresident age 16 or over and under age 18 must purchase a youth license to angle under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.475#stat.97A.475.7">97A.475, subdivision 7</a>, paragraph (a), clause (8).</p>
          <p>(c) Nonresidents age 16 or over and under age 18 may take fish by spearing without a spearing license but must possess a fishing license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.475#stat.97A.475.7">97A.475, subdivision 7</a>, paragraph (a), clause (8).</p>
          <p>(d) Nonresidents under age 16 may take fish by spearing without a spearing or angling license.</p>
          <p>(e) Limits for fish taken by spearing must comply with one of the options listed under paragraph (a).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.451.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Nonresidents under age 16 attending camps; fishing.</span></h2>
          <p>A nonresident under the age of 16 that is attending a camp conducted by a nonprofit organization may take fish by angling in adjacent and connected public waters without a license. The organization must have a certificate from the commissioner that describes the public waters where the fishing is allowed. The nonresident must possess a document, prescribed by the commissioner, for identification of the nonresident and the authorized fishing waters. The document must be signed and dated within the current calendar year by the person in charge of the camp.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.451.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/277/#laws.2.37.0">2012 c 277 art 2 s 37</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.451.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Residents 90 years of age or older; fishing.</span></h2>
          <p>A resident age 90 or older may take fish without a license.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.451.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.63.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 63</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/149/#laws.1.30.0">1987 c 149 art 1 s 30</a>,31; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/168/#laws.0.1.0">1989 c 168 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/1/#laws.0.22.0">1Sp1995 c 1 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/410/#laws.0.26.0">1996 c 410 s 26</a>,27; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/323/#laws.0.5.0">2002 c 323 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/146/#laws.0.15.0">2005 c 146 s 15</a>,16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/57/#laws.1.86.0">2007 c 57 art 1 s 86</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/131/#laws.1.28.0">2007 c 131 art 1 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/368/#laws.2.25.0">2008 c 368 art 2 s 25</a>,26; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/176/#laws.2.23.0">2009 c 176 art 2 s 23</a>,24,64; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/277/#laws.1.36.0">2012 c 277 art 1 s 36</a>-38; art 2 s 6-9,37,38; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/121/#laws.0.33.0">2013 c 121 s 33</a>-37</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.455">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.455 NONRESIDENT STUDENTS; FISHING, SMALL GAME, AND BIG GAME.</h1>
        <p>(a) A nonresident that is a full-time student at an educational institution in the state and resides in the state during the school year may obtain a resident license to take fish, small game, or big game, except moose, by providing proof of student status and residence as prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        <p>(b) A nonresident that is a full-time foreign exchange student at a high school in the state and resides with persons in the state may obtain a resident license to take big game, except moose, by providing proof of foreign exchange student status as prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.455.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.64.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 64</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/47/#laws.0.1.0">1987 c 47 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/410/#laws.0.28.0">1996 c 410 s 28</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.461">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.461 NONRESIDENT LICENSES FOR BOUNDARY WATER HUNTING OR FISHING.</h1>
        <p>Licenses to take fish or small game in or on boundary waters may be granted to nonresidents upon the same terms and conditions as licenses granted by the adjacent state or province to nonresidents of the adjacent state or province for those boundary waters. The fees for a license granted by this state may not be less than the fees for a corresponding resident license.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.461.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.65.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 65</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.465">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.465 MILITARY PERSONNEL; FISHING AND HUNTING.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.465.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Residents on leave.</span></h2>
          <p>A resident that is in the armed forces of the United States, stationed outside of the state, and in the state on leave, may hunt and fish without a license if the resident possesses official military leave papers. The resident must obtain the seals, tags, and coupons required of a licensee, which must be furnished without charge. This subdivision does not apply to the taking of moose or elk.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.465.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Spouses of residents on active military duty.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.405#stat.97A.405.5">97A.405, subdivision 5</a>, the spouse of a resident who is on active military duty may obtain resident hunting and fishing licenses.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.465.1b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.<span class="headnote">Residents discharged from active service.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A resident who has served at any time during the preceding 24 months in federal active service, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/190.05#stat.190.05.5c">190.05, subdivision 5c</a>, outside the United States as a member of the National Guard, or as a reserve component or active duty member of the United States armed forces and has been discharged from active service may take small game and fish without a license if the resident possesses official military discharge papers. The resident must obtain the seals, tags, and coupons required of a licensee, which must be furnished without charge.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The commissioner shall issue, without fee, a deer license, valid for a deer of either sex, to a resident who has served at any time during the preceding 24 months in federal active service, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/190.05#stat.190.05.5c">190.05, subdivision 5c</a>, outside the United States as a member of the National Guard, or as a reserve component or active duty member of the United States armed forces and has been discharged from active service. Eligibility under this paragraph is limited to one license per resident.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.465.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Camp Ripley personnel.</span></h2>
          <p>A nonresident who is in the military and in training at Camp Ripley may obtain a resident license to take fish.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.465.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Nonresidents stationed in state; spouses.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may issue a resident license to take game to a person in the armed forces of the United States that is stationed in the state. This paragraph does not apply to taking moose or elk.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner may issue a resident angling license to a person in the armed forces of the United States that is stationed in the state and to the spouse of a person in the armed forces of the United States that is stationed in the state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.465.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Discharged resident; obtaining deer license during season.</span></h2>
          <p>A resident who is discharged from the United States armed forces during, or within ten days before, the firearms deer season may, upon showing the official discharge paper, obtain a firearm deer license during the season that is valid immediately upon issuance.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.465.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Preference to service members.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this subdivision:</p>
          <p>(1) "qualified service member or veteran" means a Minnesota resident who:</p>
          <p>(i) is currently serving, or has served at any time during the past 24 months, in active service as a member of the United States armed forces, including the National Guard or other military reserves;</p>
          <p>(ii) has received a Purple Heart medal for qualifying military service, as shown by official military records; or</p>
          <p>(iii) has a service-connected disability rated at 100 percent as defined by the United States Department of Veterans Affairs; and</p>
          <p>(2) "active service" means service defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/190.05#stat.190.05.5b">190.05, subdivision 5b</a> or 5c.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding any other provision of this chapter, chapter 97B or 97C, or administrative rules, the commissioner may give first preference to qualified service members or veterans in any drawing or lottery involving the selection of applicants for hunting or fishing licenses, permits, and special permits. This subdivision does not apply to licenses or permits for taking moose, elk, or prairie chickens. Actions of the commissioner under this subdivision are not rules under the Administrative Procedure Act and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.386">14.386</a> does not apply.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.465.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Special hunts for military personnel.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may by rule establish criteria, special seasons, and limits for military personnel and veterans to take big game and small game by firearms or archery in designated areas or times. A person hunting under this subdivision must be participating in a hunt sponsored and administered by the Minnesota Department of Military Affairs or the Minnesota Department of Veterans Affairs.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.465.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Residents of veterans homes.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A resident from a Minnesota veterans home may obtain a firearm or muzzleloader deer license during the season and take antlerless deer without a permit in all areas of the state open during the respective regular firearms or muzzleloader deer seasons in any permit area. This subdivision does not authorize the taking of an antlerless deer by another member of a party under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97B.301#stat.97B.301.3">97B.301, subdivision 3</a>, in an area closed to taking antlerless deer or where the number of antlerless deer that may be taken is limited by a quota on the number of permits.</p>
          <p>(b) A person may assist a Minnesota veterans home resident during the firearms or muzzleloader deer season without having a deer-hunting license, but the person may not shoot a deer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.465.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Nonresident active members of National Guard; spouses.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A nonresident that is a member of the National Guard may obtain a resident license to take game. This paragraph does not apply to taking moose or elk.</p>
          <p>(b) A nonresident that is a member of the National Guard or that is the spouse of a member of the National Guard may obtain a resident license to take fish.</p>
          <p>(c) For purposes of this section, the term "member of the National Guard" means an active member of the state's National Guard or an active member of another state's National Guard who is temporarily stationed in this state.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.465.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.66.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 66</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/373/#laws.0.6.0">1987 c 373 s 6</a>,7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/384/#laws.1.9.0">1987 c 384 art 1 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/226/#laws.0.22.0">1997 c 226 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/146/#laws.0.17.0">2005 c 146 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/281/#laws.2.29.0">2006 c 281 art 2 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/57/#laws.1.87.0">2007 c 57 art 1 s 87</a>,88; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/176/#laws.2.25.0">2009 c 176 art 2 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/2/#laws.5.32.0">1Sp2011 c 2 art 5 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/1/4/#laws.5.17.0">1Sp2015 c 4 art 5 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/189/#laws.3.38.0">2016 c 189 art 3 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/60/#laws.4.52.0">2023 c 60 art 4 s 52</a>,53</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.471">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.471 NONRESIDENT COURTESY LICENSES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.471.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Game and fish officers of other jurisdictions.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may issue a courtesy nonresident license to take game or fish without charge to a game and fish or conservation employee of another state or of the United States that is in the state to assist or cooperate with the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.471.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Guests of governor or commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may issue a nonresident courtesy license to take game or fish without charge to an official of another state, the United States, or foreign country and to a representative of a conservation organization or publication that is in the state as a guest of the governor or commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.471.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Nonapplicability to moose or elk hunting.</span></h2>
          <p>This section does not apply to taking moose or elk.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.471.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.67.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 67</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/373/#laws.0.8.0">1987 c 373 s 8</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.472">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.472 PLACE OF SALE OF NONRESIDENT LICENSES; RESTRICTION.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner shall not sell or issue in any place outside this state a nonresident license to take fish in this state.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.472.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/404/#laws.0.121.0">1987 c 404 s 121</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.473">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.473 RESIDENT LIFETIME LICENSES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.473.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Resident lifetime licenses authorized.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may issue a lifetime angling license, a lifetime spearing license, a lifetime angling and spearing license, a lifetime small-game-hunting license, a lifetime firearm or archery deer-hunting license, a lifetime sporting license, or a lifetime sporting with spearing option license to a person who is a resident of the state for at least one year or who is under age 21 and the child of a person who is a resident of the state for at least one year. The license fees paid for a lifetime license are nonrefundable.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner may require the holder of a lifetime license issued under this section to notify the department each year that the license is used, by:</p>
          <p>(1) telephone or Internet notification, as specified by the commissioner;</p>
          <p>(2) the purchase of stamps for the license; or</p>
          <p>(3) registration, in the case of the resident lifetime deer license.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.473.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Lifetime angling license; fee.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A resident lifetime angling license authorizes a person to take fish by angling in the state. The license authorizes those activities authorized by the annual resident angling license. The license does not include a trout-and-salmon stamp validation, a walleye stamp validation, or other stamps required by law.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The fees for a resident lifetime angling license are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) age 3 and under, $344;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) age 4 to age 15, $469;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) age 16 to age 50, $574; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) age 51 and over, $379.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.473.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Lifetime spearing license; fee.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A resident lifetime spearing license authorizes a person to take fish by spearing in the state. The license authorizes those activities authorized by the annual resident spearing license.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The fees for a resident lifetime spearing license are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) age 3 and under, $90;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) age 4 to age 15, $124;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) age 16 to age 50, $117; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) age 51 and over, $61.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.473.2b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.<span class="headnote">Lifetime angling and spearing license; fee.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A resident lifetime angling and spearing license authorizes a person to take fish by angling or spearing in the state. The license authorizes those activities authorized by the annual resident angling and spearing licenses.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The fees for a resident lifetime angling and spearing license are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) age 3 and under, $432;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) age 4 to age 15, $579;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) age 16 to age 50, $678; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) age 51 and over, $439.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.473.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Lifetime small-game-hunting license; fee.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A resident lifetime small-game-hunting license authorizes a person to hunt and trap small game, other than wolves, in the state. The license authorizes those hunting and trapping activities authorized by the annual resident small-game-hunting license and the trapping license for fur-bearing animals other than wolves. The license does not include any hunting stamps required by law.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The fees for a resident lifetime small-game-hunting license are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) age 3 and under, $223;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) age 4 to age 15, $301;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) age 16 to age 50, $430; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) age 51 and over, $274.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.473.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Lifetime deer-hunting license; fee.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A resident lifetime deer-hunting license authorizes a person to take deer with firearms or by archery in the state. The license authorizes those activities authorized by the annual resident firearm deer-hunting license or the annual resident archery deer-hunting license. The licensee must review and confirm information each year that the license is used.</p>
          <p>(b) The fees for a resident lifetime firearm or archery deer-hunting license are:</p>
          <p>(1) age 3 and under, $458;</p>
          <p>(2) age 4 to age 15, $607;</p>
          <p>(3) age 16 to age 50, $741; and</p>
          <p>(4) age 51 and over, $528.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.473.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Lifetime sporting license; fee.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A resident lifetime sporting license authorizes a person to take fish by angling and hunt and trap small game, other than wolves, in the state. The license authorizes those activities authorized by the annual resident angling and resident small-game-hunting licenses and the resident trapping license for fur-bearing animals other than wolves. The license does not include a trout-and-salmon stamp validation, a walleye stamp validation, or any other stamps required by law.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The fees for a resident lifetime sporting license are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) age 3 and under, $522;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) age 4 to age 15, $710;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) age 16 to age 50, $927; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) age 51 and over, $603.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.473.5a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5a.<span class="headnote">Lifetime sporting with spearing option license; fee.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A resident lifetime sporting with spearing option license authorizes a person to take fish by angling or spearing and hunt and trap small game, other than wolves, in the state. The license authorizes those activities authorized by the annual resident angling, spearing, and resident small-game-hunting licenses and the resident trapping license for fur-bearing animals other than wolves. The license does not include a trout-and-salmon stamp validation, a walleye stamp validation, or any other stamps required by law.</p>
          <p>(b) The fees for a resident lifetime sporting with spearing option license are:</p>
          <p>(1) age 3 and under, $612;</p>
          <p>(2) age 4 to age 15, $833;</p>
          <p>(3) age 16 to age 50, $1,046; and</p>
          <p>(4) age 51 and over, $666.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.473.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/341/#laws.0.5.0">2000 c 341 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/2/#laws.0.108.0">1Sp2001 c 2 s 108</a>-110; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/351/#laws.0.8.0">2002 c 351 s 8</a>,9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/57/#laws.1.89.0">2007 c 57 art 1 s 89</a>,90; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/368/#laws.2.27.0">2008 c 368 art 2 s 27</a>,28; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/176/#laws.2.26.0">2009 c 176 art 2 s 26</a>-29; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/277/#laws.1.39.0">2012 c 277 art 1 s 39</a>-41; art 2 s 10-14,38; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/290/#laws.0.27.0">2014 c 290 s 27</a>-30; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/93/#laws.2.80.0">2017 c 93 art 2 s 80</a>-85; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/90/#laws.1.29.0">2024 c 90 art 1 s 29</a>-33</p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendments to subdivisions 1, 3, 4, 5, and 5a by Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, sections 29 to 33, are effective upon full implementation of the replacement electronic license system. The commissioner of natural resources must notify the revisor of statutes when the replacement electronic license system is fully implemented. Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, section 52.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.474">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.474 NONRESIDENT LIFETIME LICENSES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.474.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Nonresident lifetime licenses authorized.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may issue a lifetime angling license or a lifetime small-game-hunting license to a nonresident. The license fees paid for a lifetime license are nonrefundable.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner may require the holder of a lifetime license issued under this section to notify the department each year that the license is used, by:</p>
          <p>(1) telephone or Internet notification, as specified by the commissioner; or</p>
          <p>(2) the purchase of stamps for the license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.474.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Nonresident lifetime angling license; fee.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A nonresident lifetime angling license authorizes a person to take fish by angling in the state. The license authorizes those activities authorized by the annual nonresident angling license. The license does not include a trout-and-salmon stamp validation, a walleye stamp validation, or other stamps required by law.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The fees for a nonresident lifetime angling license are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) age 3 and under, $821;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) age 4 to age 15, $1,046;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) age 16 to age 50, $1,191; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) age 51 and over, $794.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.474.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Nonresident lifetime small-game-hunting license; fee.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A nonresident lifetime small-game-hunting license authorizes a person to hunt small game in the state. The license authorizes those hunting activities authorized by the annual nonresident small-game-hunting license. The license does not include any hunting stamps required by law.</p>
          <p>(b) The fees for a nonresident lifetime small-game-hunting license are:</p>
          <p>(1) age 3 and under, $947;</p>
          <p>(2) age 4 to age 15, $1,280;</p>
          <p>(3) age 16 to age 50, $1,633; and</p>
          <p>(4) age 51 and over, $1,083.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.474.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/341/#laws.0.6.0">2000 c 341 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/2/#laws.0.111.0">1Sp2001 c 2 s 111</a>,112; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/368/#laws.2.29.0">2008 c 368 art 2 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/277/#laws.2.15.0">2012 c 277 art 2 s 15</a>,38; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/93/#laws.2.86.0">2017 c 93 art 2 s 86</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/90/#laws.1.34.0">2024 c 90 art 1 s 34</a></p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendment to subdivision 3 by Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, section 34, is effective upon full implementation of the replacement electronic license system. The commissioner of natural resources must notify the revisor of statutes when the replacement electronic license system is fully implemented. Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, section 52.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.4742">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.4742 LIFETIME FISH AND WILDLIFE TRUST FUND.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.4742.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Establishment; purpose.</span></h2>
          <p>The lifetime fish and wildlife trust fund is established as a fund in the state treasury. All money received from the issuance of lifetime angling, spearing, angling and spearing, small-game hunting, deer hunting, sporting, and sporting with spearing option licenses and earnings on the fund shall be credited to the lifetime fish and wildlife trust fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.4742.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Investment; use.</span></h2>
          <p>Money in the lifetime fish and wildlife trust fund shall be invested by the State Investment Board to secure the maximum return consistent with the maintenance of the perpetuity of the fund. The income received and accruing from investments of the fund shall be deposited in the lifetime fish and wildlife trust fund. Each year the commissioner of management and budget shall transfer from the lifetime fish and wildlife trust fund to the game and fish fund an amount equal to the amount that would otherwise have been collected from annual license fees for each lifetime license. Surcharge amounts shall be transferred based on sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.071#stat.97A.071.2">97A.071, subdivision 2</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.075#stat.97A.075.1">97A.075, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.4742.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/290/#laws.0.70.0">2014 c 290 s 70</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.4742.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/272/#laws.0.98.0">2012 c 272 s 98</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.4742.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/341/#laws.0.7.0">2000 c 341 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/351/#laws.0.10.0">2002 c 351 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/255/#laws.0.30.0">2004 c 255 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.99.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 99</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/176/#laws.2.30.0">2009 c 176 art 2 s 30</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.475">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.475 LICENSE FEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirements for issuance.</span></h2>
          <p>A license shall be issued when the requirements of the law are met and the license fee specified in this section is paid.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Resident hunting.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Fees for the following licenses, to be issued to residents only, are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) for persons age 18 or over and under age 65 to take small game, $15.50;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) for persons age 65 or over, $7 to take small game;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) for persons age 18 or over to take turkey, $26;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) for persons age 13 or over and under age 18 to take turkey, $5;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) for persons age 18 or over to take deer with firearms during the regular firearms season, $34;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) for persons age 18 or over to take deer by archery, $34;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) for persons age 18 or over to take deer by muzzleloader during the muzzleloader season, $34;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) to take moose, for a party of not more than six persons, $356;</p>
          <p class="in">(9) for persons age 18 or over to take bear, $44;</p>
          <p class="in">(10) to take elk, for a party of not more than two persons, $287;</p>
          <p>(11) to take light geese during the light goose conservation order, $2.50;</p>
          <p>(12) to take sandhill crane during the sandhill crane season, $3;</p>
          <p class="in">(13) to take prairie chickens, $23;</p>
          <p class="in">(14) for persons age 13 or over and under age 18 to take deer with firearms during the regular firearms season, $5;</p>
          <p class="in">(15) for persons age 13 or over and under age 18 to take deer by archery, $5;</p>
          <p class="in">(16) for persons age 13 or over and under age 18 to take deer by muzzleloader during the muzzleloader season, $5;</p>
          <p>(17) for persons age 10, 11, or 12 to take bear, no fee;</p>
          <p>(18) for persons age 13 or over and under age 18 to take bear, $5;</p>
          <p>(19) for persons age 18 or over to take small game for a consecutive 72-hour period selected by the licensee, $19, of which an amount equal to one-half of the fee for the migratory-waterfowl stamp under subdivision 5, clause (1), shall be deposited in the waterfowl habitat improvement account under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.075#stat.97A.075.2">97A.075, subdivision 2</a>; one-half of the fee for the pheasant stamp under subdivision 5, clause (2), shall be deposited in the pheasant habitat improvement account under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.075#stat.97A.075.4">97A.075, subdivision 4</a>; and one-half of the small-game surcharge under subdivision 4, shall be deposited in the wildlife acquisition account;</p>
          <p>(20) for persons age 16 or over and under age 18 to take small game, $5;</p>
          <p>(21) to take wolf, $30;</p>
          <p>(22) for persons age 12 and under to take turkey, no fee;</p>
          <p>(23) for persons age 10, 11, or 12 to take deer by firearm, no fee;</p>
          <p>(24) for persons age 10, 11, or 12 to take deer by archery, no fee; and</p>
          <p>(25) for persons age 10, 11, or 12 to take deer by muzzleloader during the muzzleloader season, no fee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Nonresident hunting.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Fees for the following licenses, to be issued to nonresidents, are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) for persons age 18 or over to take small game, $90.50;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) for persons age 18 or over to take deer with firearms during the regular firearms season, $180;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) for persons age 18 or over to take deer by archery, $180;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) for persons age 18 or over to take deer by muzzleloader during the muzzleloader season, $180;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) for persons age 18 or over to take bear, $225;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) for persons age 18 or over to take turkey, $91;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) for persons age 13 or over and under age 18 to take turkey, $5;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) to take raccoon or bobcat, $178;</p>
          <p>(9) to take light geese during the light goose conservation order, $2.50;</p>
          <p>(10) to take sandhill crane during the sandhill crane season, $3;</p>
          <p class="in">(11) for persons age 13 or over and under age 18 to take deer with firearms during the regular firearms season in any open season option or time period, $5;</p>
          <p class="in">(12) for persons age 13 or over and under age 18 to take deer by archery, $5;</p>
          <p class="in">(13) for persons age 13 or over and under age 18 to take deer during the muzzleloader season, $5;</p>
          <p>(14) for persons age 13 or over and under 18 to take bear, $5;</p>
          <p>(15) for persons age 18 or over to take small game for a consecutive 72-hour period selected by the licensee, $75, of which an amount equal to one-half of the fee for the migratory-waterfowl stamp under subdivision 5, clause (1), shall be deposited in the waterfowl habitat improvement account under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.075#stat.97A.075.2">97A.075, subdivision 2</a>; one-half of the fee for the pheasant stamp under subdivision 5, clause (2), shall be deposited in the pheasant habitat improvement account under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.075#stat.97A.075.4">97A.075, subdivision 4</a>; and one-half of the small-game surcharge under subdivision 4, shall be deposited into the wildlife acquisition account;</p>
          <p>(16) for persons age 16 or 17 to take small game, $5;</p>
          <p>(17) to take wolf, $250;</p>
          <p>(18) for persons age 12 and under to take turkey, no fee;</p>
          <p>(19) for persons age 10, 11, or 12 to take deer by firearm, no fee;</p>
          <p>(20) for persons age 10, 11, or 12 to take deer by archery, no fee;</p>
          <p>(21) for persons age 10, 11, or 12 to take deer by muzzleloader during the muzzleloader season, no fee; and</p>
          <p>(22) for persons age 10, 11, or 12 to take bear, no fee.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) A $5 surcharge shall be added to nonresident hunting licenses issued under paragraph (a), clauses (1) to (6) and (8). An additional commission may not be assessed on this surcharge.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Deer license donation and surcharge.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A person may agree to add a donation of $1, $3, or $5 to the fees for annual resident and nonresident licenses to take deer by firearms or archery established under subdivisions 2, clauses (5), (6), (7), (15), (16), and (17), and 3, paragraph (a), clauses (2), (3), (4), (12), (13), and (14).</p>
          <p>(b) Beginning March 1, 2008, fees for bonus licenses to take deer by firearms or archery established under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97B.301#stat.97B.301.4">97B.301, subdivision 4</a>, must be increased by a surcharge of $1.</p>
          <p>(c) An additional commission may not be assessed on the donation or surcharge.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Small-game surcharge and donation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Fees for annual licenses to take small game must be increased by a surcharge of $6.50, except licenses under subdivisions 2, clauses (20) and (21); and 3, paragraph (a), clauses (16) and (17). An additional commission may not be assessed on the surcharge and the following statement must be included in the annual small-game-hunting regulations: "This $6.50 surcharge is being paid by hunters for the acquisition and development of wildlife lands."</p>
          <p>(b) A person may agree to add a donation of $1, $3, or $5 to the fees for annual resident and nonresident licenses to take small game. An additional commission may not be assessed on the donation. The following statement must be included in the annual small-game-hunting regulations: "The small-game license donations are being paid by hunters for administration of the walk-in access program."</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Hunting stamps.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Fees for the following stamps and stamp validations are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) migratory-waterfowl stamp, $7.50; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) pheasant stamp, $7.50.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Resident fishing.</span></h2>
          <p>Fees for the following licenses, to be issued to residents only, are:</p>
          <p>(1) for persons age 18 or over to take fish by angling, $25;</p>
          <p>(2) for persons age 18 or over to take fish by angling, for a combined license for a married couple, $40;</p>
          <p>(3) for persons age 18 or over to take fish by spearing from a dark house, $6, and the person must possess an angling license;</p>
          <p>(4) for persons age 18 or over to take fish by angling for a 24-hour period selected by the licensee, $12;</p>
          <p>(5) for persons age 18 or over to take fish by angling for a consecutive 72-hour period selected by the licensee, $14;</p>
          <p>(6) for persons age 18 or over to take fish by angling for three consecutive years, $71; and</p>
          <p>(7) for persons age 16 or over and under age 18 to take fish by angling, $5.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Nonresident fishing.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Fees for the following licenses, to be issued to nonresidents, are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) for persons age 18 or over to take fish by angling, $46;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) for persons age 18 or over to take fish by angling limited to seven consecutive days selected by the licensee, $38;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) for persons age 18 or over to take fish by angling for a consecutive 72-hour period selected by the licensee, $31;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) for persons age 18 or over to take fish by angling for a combined license for a family for one or both parents and dependent children under the age of 16, $63;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) for persons age 18 or over to take fish by angling for a 24-hour period selected by the licensee, $14;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) to take fish by angling for a combined license for a married couple, limited to 14 consecutive days selected by one of the licensees, $49;</p>
          <p>(7) for persons age 18 or over to take fish by spearing from a dark house, $12, and the person must possess an angling license; and</p>
          <p>(8) for persons age 16 or over and under age 18 to take fish by angling, $5.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) A $5 surcharge shall be added to all nonresident fishing licenses, except licenses issued under paragraph (a), clauses (5) and (8). An additional commission may not be assessed on this surcharge.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Minnesota sporting; supersports.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall issue Minnesota sporting licenses to residents only. The licensee may take fish by angling and small game. The fee for the license is:</p>
          <p>(1) for an individual, $34.50; and</p>
          <p>(2) for a combined license for a married couple to take fish and for one spouse to take small game, $50.50.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner shall issue Minnesota supersports licenses to residents only. The licensee may take fish by angling, including trout; small game, including pheasant and waterfowl; and deer by firearms or muzzleloader or by archery. The fee for the supersports license, including all required stamp validations is:</p>
          <p>(1) for an individual age 18 or over, $93.50; and</p>
          <p>(2) for a combined license for a married couple to take fish, including the trout-and-salmon stamp validation, and for one spouse to take small game, including pheasant and waterfowl, and deer, $119.50.</p>
          <p>(c) Revenue for the stamp endorsements under paragraph (b) shall be deposited according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.075">97A.075, subdivisions 2</a>, 3, and 4.</p>
          <p>(d) Revenue for the deer license endorsement under paragraph (b) shall be deposited according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.075#stat.97A.075.1">97A.075, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/561/#laws.0.28.0">1994 c 561 s 28</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Trout-and-salmon stamp validation.</span></h2>
          <p>The fee for a trout-and-salmon stamp validation is $10.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.10a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10a.<span class="headnote">Walleye stamp validation.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A person may agree to purchase a walleye stamp validation for $5.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Fish houses, dark houses, and shelters; residents.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Fees for the following licenses are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) annual for a fish house, dark house, or shelter that is not rented, $15;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) annual for a fish house, dark house, or shelter that is rented, $30;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) three-year for a fish house, dark house, or shelter that is not rented, $42; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) three-year for a fish house, dark house, or shelter that is rented, $87.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Fish houses, dark houses, and shelters; nonresident.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Fees for fish house, dark house, and shelter licenses for a nonresident are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) annual, $37;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) seven consecutive days selected by the licensee, $21; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) three-year, $111.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Netting whitefish and ciscoes for personal consumption.</span></h2>
          <p>The fee for a license to net whitefish and ciscoes in inland lakes and international waters for personal consumption is, for each net, $10.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/216/#laws.0.83.0">1997 c 216 s 83</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Fishing guides.</span></h2>
          <p>The fee for a license to operate a charter boat and guide anglers on Lake Superior or the St. Louis River Estuary is:</p>
          <p>(1) for a resident, $125;</p>
          <p>(2) for a nonresident, $400; or</p>
          <p>(3) if another state charges a Minnesota resident a fee greater than $440 for a Lake Superior or St. Louis River Estuary fishing guide license in that state, the nonresident fee for a resident of that state is that greater fee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Resident bear-hunting outfitters.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The fee for a resident bear-hunting-outfitter license is $82.50 and is available only to a Minnesota resident individual.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The fee for a resident master bear-hunting-outfitter license is $165. The fee to add an additional person under the license is $82.50 per person.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/623/#laws.1.47.0">1994 c 623 art 1 s 47</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Shooting preserves.</span></h2>
          <p>The fee for a shooting preserve license is:</p>
          <p>(1) for a private shooting preserve, $100; and</p>
          <p>(2) for a commercial shooting preserve, $500.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Taxidermists.</span></h2>
          <p>The fee for a taxidermist license, to be issued for a three-year period to residents only, is:</p>
          <p>(1) for persons age 18 and older, $44; and</p>
          <p>(2) for persons under age 18, $27.50.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Trapping licenses.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The fee for a license to trap fur-bearing animals, other than wolves, is:</p>
          <p>(1) for residents over age 13 and under age 18, $5;</p>
          <p>(2) for residents age 18 or over and under age 65, $23;</p>
          <p>(3) for residents age 65 or over, $11.50; and</p>
          <p>(4) for nonresidents, $84.</p>
          <p>(b) The fee for a license to trap wolves is $30, to be issued to residents only.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.21">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">Fur buying and selling; residents.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The fee for a license for a resident to buy and sell raw furs is $110.</p>
          <p>(b) The fee for a supplemental license to buy and sell furs is $55.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.22">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.<span class="headnote">Fur buying and selling; nonresidents.</span></h2>
          <p>The fee for a license for a nonresident to buy and sell raw furs is $500.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.23">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 23.<span class="headnote">Raw-fur tanning.</span></h2>
          <p>The fee for a license to tan and dress raw furs to be issued to residents and nonresidents is $16.50.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.24">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 24.<span class="headnote">Game and fur farms.</span></h2>
          <p>The fee for a game and fur farm license is $16.50.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.25">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 25.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/1/4/#laws.5.34.0">1Sp2015 c 4 art 5 s 34</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.26">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 26.<span class="headnote">Minnow dealers.</span></h2>
          <p>The fees for the following licenses are:</p>
          <p>(1) minnow dealer, $310;</p>
          <p>(2) minnow dealer's vehicle, $15;</p>
          <p>(3) exporting minnow dealer for residents and nonresidents, $700; and</p>
          <p>(4) exporting minnow dealer's vehicle for residents and nonresidents, $15.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.27">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 27.<span class="headnote">Minnow retailers.</span></h2>
          <p>The fees for the following licenses, to be issued to residents and nonresidents, are:</p>
          <p>(1) minnow retailer, $47; and</p>
          <p>(2) minnow retailer's vehicle, $15.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.28">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 28.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/255/#laws.0.51.0">2004 c 255 s 51</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.29">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 29.<span class="headnote">Private fish hatcheries.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The fees for the following licenses to be issued to residents and nonresidents are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) for a private fish hatchery, with annual sales under $200, $70;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) for a private fish hatchery, with annual sales of $200 or more, $210 for the base license. The commissioner must establish an additional fee based on the acreage of the operation. Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.1283">16A.1283</a>, the commissioner may, by written order published in the State Register, establish the additional fee required by this subdivision. The fee is not subject to the rulemaking provisions of chapter 14 and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.386">14.386 </a>does not apply; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) to take sucker eggs from public waters for a private fish hatchery, $400, plus $6 for each quart in excess of 100 quarts.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.29a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 29a.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/566/#laws.0.23.0">1992 c 566 s 23</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.30">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 30.<span class="headnote">Commercial netting of fish.</span></h2>
          <p>The fees to take commercial fish are:</p>
          <p>(1) commercial license fees:</p>
          <p>(i) for residents and nonresidents seining and netting in inland waters, $120;</p>
          <p>(ii) for residents netting in Lake Superior, $120;</p>
          <p>(iii) for residents netting in Lake of the Woods, Rainy, Namakan, and Sand Point Lakes, $120;</p>
          <p>(iv) for residents seining in the Mississippi River from St. Anthony Falls to the St. Croix River junction, $120;</p>
          <p>(v) for residents seining, netting, and set lining in Wisconsin boundary waters from Lake St. Croix to the Iowa border, $120; and</p>
          <p>(vi) for a resident apprentice license, $55; and</p>
          <p>(2) commercial gear fees:</p>
          <p>(i) for each gill net in Lake Superior, Wisconsin boundary waters, and Namakan Lake, $5 per 100 feet of net;</p>
          <p>(ii) for each seine in inland waters, on the Mississippi River as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97C.801#stat.97C.801.2">97C.801, subdivision 2</a>, and in Wisconsin boundary waters, $9 per 100 feet;</p>
          <p>(iii) for each commercial hoop net in inland waters, $2;</p>
          <p>(iv) for each submerged fyke, trap, and hoop net in Lake Superior, St. Louis Estuary, Lake of the Woods, and Rainy, Namakan, and Sand Point Lakes, and for each pound net in Lake Superior, $20;</p>
          <p>(v) for each stake and pound net in Lake of the Woods, $90; and</p>
          <p>(vi) for each set line in the Wisconsin boundary waters, $45.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.31">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 31.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/216/#laws.0.83.0">1997 c 216 s 83</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.32">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 32.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/216/#laws.0.83.0">1997 c 216 s 83</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.33">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 33.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/216/#laws.0.83.0">1997 c 216 s 83</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.34">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 34.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/216/#laws.0.83.0">1997 c 216 s 83</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.35">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 35.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/216/#laws.0.83.0">1997 c 216 s 83</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.36">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 36.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/216/#laws.0.83.0">1997 c 216 s 83</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.37">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 37.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/216/#laws.0.83.0">1997 c 216 s 83</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.38">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 38.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/131/#laws.1.96.0">2007 c 131 art 1 s 96</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.39">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 39.<span class="headnote">Fish packer.</span></h2>
          <p>The fee for a license to prepare dressed game fish or native rough fish for transportation or shipment is $40.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.40">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 40.<span class="headnote">Fish vendors.</span></h2>
          <p>The fee for a license to use a motor vehicle to sell fish is $70.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.41">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 41.<span class="headnote">Turtle license.</span></h2>
          <p> The fee for a recreational turtle license to take, transport, and possess turtles for personal use is $25.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.42">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 42.<span class="headnote">Frog dealers.</span></h2>
          <p>The fee for the licenses to deal in frogs that are to be used for purposes other than bait are:</p>
          <p>(1) for a resident to purchase, possess, and transport frogs, $220;</p>
          <p>(2) for a nonresident to purchase, possess, and transport frogs, $550; and</p>
          <p>(3) for a resident to take, possess, transport, and sell frogs, $35.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.43">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 43.<span class="headnote">Duplicate licenses.</span></h2>
          <p>The fees for duplicate licenses are:</p>
          <p>(1) for licenses to take big game, $5, except licenses issued under subdivision 8, paragraph (b); and</p>
          <p>(2) for other licenses, $2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.44">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 44.<span class="headnote">Replacement licenses.</span></h2>
          <p>The fee for a replacement deer or turkey license is $5, except there is no fee for replacing a deer license issued under subdivision 8, paragraph (b).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.475.45">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 45.<span class="headnote">Camp Ripley archery deer hunt.</span></h2>
          <p>The application fee for the Camp Ripley archery deer hunt is $14.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.475.history
			">
