      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.481">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.481 LICENSE APPLICATIONS; PENALTY.</h1>
        <p>All information required on a license application form must be furnished. The applicant is subject to the penalty prescribed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.301#stat.97A.301.1">97A.301, subdivision 1</a>, clause (5).</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.481.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.69.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 69</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/149/#laws.1.33.0">1987 c 149 art 1 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/287/#laws.0.3.0">1989 c 287 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/90/#laws.1.35.0">2024 c 90 art 1 s 35</a></p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendment to this section by Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, section 35, is effective upon full implementation of the replacement electronic license system. The commissioner of natural resources must notify the revisor of statutes when the replacement electronic license system is fully implemented. Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, section 52.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.482">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.482 LICENSE APPLICATIONS; COLLECTING SOCIAL SECURITY NUMBERS.</h1>
        <p>(a) All applicants for individual noncommercial game and fish licenses under this chapter and chapters 97B and 97C must include the applicant's Social Security number on the license application. If an applicant does not have a Social Security number, the applicant must certify that the applicant does not have a Social Security number.</p>
        <p>(b) The Social Security numbers collected by the commissioner on game and fish license applications are private data under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.355#stat.13.355.1">13.355, subdivision 1</a>, and must be provided by the commissioner to the commissioner of children, youth, and families for child support enforcement purposes. Title IV-D of the Social Security Act, United States Code, title 42, section 666(a)(13), requires the collection of Social Security numbers on game and fish license applications for child support enforcement purposes.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.482.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/14/#laws.10.2.0">1Sp2003 c 14 art 10 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/228/#laws.1.22.0">2004 c 228 art 1 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.101.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 101</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/277/#laws.1.48.0">2012 c 277 art 1 s 48</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.8.70.0">2024 c 80 art 8 s 70</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.485">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.485 ISSUANCE OF LICENSES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall issue and sell licenses. The commissioner shall furnish licenses and applications to agents authorized to issue licenses.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/513/#laws.3.79.0">1992 c 513 art 3 s 79</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/255/#laws.0.51.0">2004 c 255 s 51</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Licenses to take additional deer.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may appoint federal, state, or local government employees to be agents of the commissioner for the sale of licenses or permits to take additional deer under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97B.301#stat.97B.301.4">97B.301, subdivision 4</a>. A bond is not required of a government employee appointed under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Appointing agents.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may appoint agents of the commissioner to issue and sell licenses. The appointment may be revoked by the commissioner at any time.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Application to sell licenses by agent.</span></h2>
          <p>To be an agent, a person must apply to the commissioner in writing and in a manner approved by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Agents responsible for fees.</span></h2>
          <p>An agent must promptly deposit and remit all money received from the sale of licenses, except issuing fees, to the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Licenses to be sold and issuing fees.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Persons authorized to sell licenses under this section must issue the following licenses for the license fee and the following issuing fees:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) to take deer or bear with firearms and by archery, the issuing fee is $1;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) Minnesota sporting, the issuing fee is $1;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) to take small game, to take fish by angling or by spearing, and to trap fur-bearing animals, the issuing fee is $1;</p>
          <p>(4) to apply for a limited hunt drawing, the issuing fee is $1 unless the application requires a license purchase at the time of application and the license purchase requires an application fee;</p>
          <p>(5) for a prairie-chicken license, the issuing fee is $1;</p>
          <p>(6) for a turkey license, the issuing fee is $1;</p>
          <p>(7) for an elk license, the issuing fee is $1;</p>
          <p>(8) for a moose license, the issuing fee is $1;</p>
          <p>(9) for a wolf license, the issuing fee is $1;</p>
          <p class="in">(10) for a stamp validation that is not issued simultaneously with a license, an issuing fee of 50 cents may be charged at the discretion of the authorized seller;</p>
          <p class="in">(11) for stamp validations issued simultaneously with a license, there is no fee;</p>
          <p class="in">(12) for licenses, seals, tags, or coupons issued without a fee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.441">97A.441, subdivisions 1 </a>to 6a, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.465">97A.465</a>, there is no fee;</p>
          <p class="in">(13) for lifetime licenses, there is no fee; and</p>
          <p class="in">(14) for all other licenses, permits, renewals, or applications or any other transaction through the electronic licensing system under this chapter or any other chapter when an issuing fee is not specified, an issuing fee of $1 may be charged at the discretion of the authorized seller.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Only one issuing fee may be collected when selling more than one stamp in the same transaction after the end of the season for which the stamp was issued.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The agent shall keep the issuing fee as a commission for selling the licenses.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The commissioner shall collect the issuing fee on licenses sold by the commissioner.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) A license, except stamps, must state the amount of the issuing fee and that the issuing fee is kept by the seller as a commission for selling the licenses.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) For duplicate licenses, including licenses issued without a fee, the issuing fees are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) for licenses to take big game, 75 cents; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) for other licenses, 50 cents.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Electronic licensing system; commission.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The commissioner shall retain for the operation of the electronic licensing system the commission established under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.027#stat.84.027.15">84.027, subdivision 15</a>, and issuing fees collected by the commissioner on all license fees.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/255/#laws.0.51.0">2004 c 255 s 51</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Certain licenses not to be issued after season opens.</span></h2>
          <p>A license to guide bear hunters may not be issued after the day before the opening of the related firearms season.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/255/#laws.0.51.0">2004 c 255 s 51</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Rules for accounting and procedures.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall prescribe rules for the accounting and procedural requirements necessary to assure the efficient handling of licenses and license fees. The commissioner may, by rule, establish standards for the appointment and revocation of agents to assure the efficient distribution of licenses throughout the state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/277/#laws.1.91.0">2012 c 277 art 1 s 91</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.485.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">One-day paper fishing licenses.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner must allow one-day paper fishing licenses to be sold by fishing guides operating charter boats.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.485.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.70.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 70</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/404/#laws.0.132.0">1987 c 404 s 132</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/437/#laws.0.3.0">1988 c 437 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/287/#laws.0.4.0">1989 c 287 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.1.124.0">1989 c 335 art 1 s 124</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/608/#laws.7.1.0">1990 c 608 art 7 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/227/#laws.0.1.0">1991 c 227 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/254/#laws.2.33.0">1991 c 254 art 2 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/259/#laws.0.23.0">1991 c 259 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/462/#laws.0.14.0">1992 c 462 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/231/#laws.0.28.0">1993 c 231 s 28</a>,29; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/310/#laws.0.5.0">1993 c 310 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/561/#laws.0.21.0">1994 c 561 s 21</a>,22; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/623/#laws.1.25.0">1994 c 623 art 1 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/632/#laws.2.25.0">1994 c 632 art 2 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/226/#laws.0.25.0">1997 c 226 s 25</a>-27; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/463/#laws.0.14.0">2000 c 463 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/2/#laws.0.116.0">1Sp2001 c 2 s 116</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/376/#laws.0.9.0">2002 c 376 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/128/#laws.1.74.0">2003 c 128 art 1 s 74</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/255/#laws.0.32.0">2004 c 255 s 32</a>-37; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/146/#laws.0.20.0">2005 c 146 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.102.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 102</a>,103; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/57/#laws.1.96.0">2007 c 57 art 1 s 96</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/368/#laws.2.36.0">2008 c 368 art 2 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/277/#laws.2.28.0">2012 c 277 art 2 s 28</a>,38; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/121/#laws.0.41.0">2013 c 121 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/290/#laws.0.33.0">2014 c 290 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/90/#laws.1.36.0">2024 c 90 art 1 s 36</a></p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendment to subdivision 6 by Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, section 36, is effective upon full implementation of the replacement electronic license system. The commissioner of natural resources must notify the revisor of statutes when the replacement electronic license system is fully implemented. Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, section 52.</p>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>Subdivision 13 is repealed by Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, section 51, effective upon full implementation of the replacement electronic license system. The commissioner of natural resources must notify the revisor of statutes when the replacement electronic license system is fully implemented. Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, section 52.</p>
      <h1 class="fgh">POSSESSING AND TRANSPORTING<br>WILD ANIMALS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.501">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.501 WILD ANIMALS; GENERAL RESTRICTIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.501.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">General restrictions.</span></h2>
          <p>A person may not take, buy, sell, transport, or possess a protected wild animal unless allowed by the game and fish laws. The ownership of all wild animals is in the state, unless the wild animal has been lawfully acquired under the game and fish laws. The ownership of a wild animal that is lawfully acquired reverts to the state if a law relating to sale, transportation, or possession of the wild animal is violated.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.501.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Endangered species.</span></h2>
          <p>A person may not take, import, transport, or sell an endangered species of wild animal, or sell, or possess with intent to sell an article made from the parts of a wild animal, except as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.0895">84.0895</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.501.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Contraceptive chemicals.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A person may not administer contraceptive chemicals to noncaptive wild animals without a permit issued by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner shall adopt rules establishing standards and guidelines for the administration of contraceptive chemicals to noncaptive wild animals. The rules may specify chemical delivery methods and devices and monitoring requirements.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.501.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.71.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 71</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/384/#laws.1.10.0">1987 c 384 art 1 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/623/#laws.1.26.0">1994 c 623 art 1 s 26</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.502">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.502 DEER KILLED BY MOTOR VEHICLES.</h1>
        <p>(a) Deer killed by a motor vehicle on a public road must be removed by the road authority, as defined by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/160.02#stat.160.02.25">160.02, subdivision 25</a>, unless the driver of the motor vehicle is allowed to possess the deer under paragraph (b).</p>
        <p>(b) The driver of a motor vehicle that has collided with and killed a deer on a public road has priority for a possession permit for the entire deer if the facts indicate that the deer was not taken illegally.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.502.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/404/#laws.0.133.0">1987 c 404 s 133</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/437/#laws.0.4.0">1988 c 437 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/2/#laws.5.34.0">1Sp2011 c 2 art 5 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/290/#laws.0.34.0">2014 c 290 s 34</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.505">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.505 POSSESSING WILD ANIMALS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.505.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/149/#laws.1.54.0">1987 c 149 art 1 s 54</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.505.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Unlawfully obtained or shipped animals brought into state.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A person may not possess a wild animal that has been unlawfully taken, bought, sold, or possessed outside the state, or unlawfully shipped into the state.</p>
          <p>(b) When entering the state from Canada, a person who possesses fish that were unlawfully taken or possessed under paragraph (a) may be charged in the same manner as for possessing fish that were unlawfully taken or possessed in the state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.505.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/149/#laws.1.54.0">1987 c 149 art 1 s 54</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.505.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Transporting animals into state.</span></h2>
          <p>Wild animals lawfully taken, bought, sold, or possessed outside the state may be brought or shipped into the state unless otherwise provided by law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.505.3b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3b.<span class="headnote">Wild animals taken on Red Lake Reservation lands.</span></h2>
          <p>Wild animals taken and tagged on the Red Lake Reservation lands in accordance with the Red Lake Band's Conservation Code and all applicable federal law are considered lawfully taken and possessed under state law. Possessing wild animals harvested under this subdivision is in addition to any state limits.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.505.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Storing protected wild animals.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A person that stores protected wild animals for others must plainly mark the package, in ink, with the name and address of the owner, the license number of the person taking the animal, and the number and species in the package.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.505.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Animals acquired by gift.</span></h2>
          <p>Lawfully taken protected wild animals may be transferred by gift. A person is not required to have a license to possess and transport protected wild animals acquired by gift.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.505.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/149/#laws.1.54.0">1987 c 149 art 1 s 54</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.505.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Exceptions.</span></h2>
          <p>This section does not apply to mounted specimens of wild animals, antlers, tanned hides, and dressed furs lawfully taken.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.505.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Importing Cervidae carcasses.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Importing Cervidae carcasses procured by any means into Minnesota is prohibited except for:</p>
          <p>(1) cut and wrapped meat;</p>
          <p>(2) quarters or other portions of meat with no part of the spinal column or head attached;</p>
          <p>(3) antlers, hides, or teeth;</p>
          <p>(4) if cleaned of all brain tissue, antlers attached to skull caps or whole skulls; and</p>
          <p>(5) finished taxidermy mounts.</p>
          <p>(b) Cervidae carcasses originating from outside Minnesota may be transported on a direct route through the state by nonresidents.</p>
          <p>(c) Heads from cervids with or without the cape and neck attached that originate from outside Minnesota may be transported into Minnesota only if they are delivered to a licensed taxidermist within 48 hours of entering Minnesota.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.505.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Possessing live Cervidae.</span></h2>
          <p>A person may not possess live Cervidae, except as authorized in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/17.452">17.452</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/35.153">35.153</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/35.155">35.155</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.401">97A.401</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.505.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.72.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 72</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/149/#laws.1.34.0">1987 c 149 art 1 s 34</a>,35; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/231/#laws.0.30.0">1993 c 231 s 30</a>,31; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/406/#laws.0.1.0">2000 c 406 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/128/#laws.1.75.0">2003 c 128 art 1 s 75</a>,76; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/215/#laws.0.16.0">2004 c 215 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.1.97.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 1 s 97</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/131/#laws.1.31.0">2007 c 131 art 1 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/2/#laws.5.35.0">1Sp2011 c 2 art 5 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/4/#laws.3.53.0">1Sp2019 c 4 art 3 s 53</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/6/#laws.2.61.0">1Sp2021 c 6 art 2 s 61</a>,62; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/116/#laws.3.35.0">2024 c 116 art 3 s 35</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.510">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.510 GAME FOR CONSUMPTION AT FUNDRAISING EVENTS.</h1>
        <p>(a) Nonprofit organizations may charge a fee for admission to fundraising events when lawfully taken and possessed game, excluding migratory game birds, as defined in Code of Federal Regulations, title 50, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/20.11">20.11</a>, that cannot be sold under federal law, is donated to the organization and is served for consumption on the premises where the fundraising event is held.</p>
        <p>(b) Game donated to the nonprofit organization must be marked as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.505#stat.97A.505.4">97A.505, subdivision 4</a>. The game donated to a nonprofit organization and stored by the organization is considered to be in the possession of the person making the donation, and is subject to inspection as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.215#stat.97A.215.1">97A.215, subdivision 1</a>. As provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.505#stat.97A.505.5">97A.505, subdivision 5</a>, a license is not required for the nonprofit organization to possess or transport the donated game.</p>
        <p>(c) The nonprofit organization must keep records of the game donated to the organization, and the records must be available for inspection for two years from the date of the fundraising event. The records must show:</p>
        <p>(1) the names and addresses of persons donating the game;</p>
        <p>(2) the license number or possession permit number under which the game was lawfully taken or possessed; and</p>
        <p>(3) the date, location, and purpose of the fundraising event that utilized the donation.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.510.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/351/#laws.0.13.0">2002 c 351 s 13</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.511">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.511 FUR-BEARING ANIMALS.</h1>
        <p class="in">The skins of fur-bearing animals and the flesh of beaver, muskrat, raccoon, rabbits and hares, legally taken and bearing the required seals or tags required by the game and fish laws, may be bought, sold, and transported at any time.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.511.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.73.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 73</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/131/#laws.1.32.0">2007 c 131 art 1 s 32</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.512">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.512 SALE OF INEDIBLE PORTIONS OF BIG GAME ANIMALS, FUR-BEARING ANIMALS, FISH, AND GAME BIRDS OTHER THAN MIGRATORY WATERFOWL.</h1>
        <p>(a) Except as otherwise provided by the game and fish laws and as restricted in this section, a person may possess, transport, buy, or sell the following inedible portions of lawfully taken or acquired big game animals, fur-bearing animals, fish, and game birds other than migratory waterfowl: bones, including skulls; sinews; adipose tissue, hides, and skins; hooves; teeth; claws; and antlers.</p>
        <p>(b) A person may not buy or sell bear paws, unless attached to the hide, or bear gallbladders.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.512.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/589/#laws.0.3.0">1992 c 589 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/185/#laws.0.29.0">2001 c 185 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/116/#laws.3.36.0">2024 c 116 art 3 s 36</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.515">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.515 PELTS, SKINS, AND HIDES TAKEN ON INDIAN RESERVATIONS.</h1>
        <p>The pelts, skins, and hides of protected wild animals taken on an Indian reservation in this state, except the Fond du Lac Reservation, may be transported, sold, and disposed of as prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.515.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.74.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 74</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.521">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.521 TRANSPORTING WILD ANIMALS; GENERALLY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.521.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">General authority; residents.</span></h2>
          <p>A resident may transport wild animals to any place in the state if the resident and the animals are in the same vehicle.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.521.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">General authority; nonresidents.</span></h2>
          <p>A nonresident may transport wild animals taken in the state if the nonresident and the animals are in the same vehicle.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.521.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Wild animals in containers.</span></h2>
          <p>A person that transports wild animals in a container must mark or identify the container as prescribed under the game and fish laws or by commissioner's rule.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.521.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Animals that may be lawfully sold.</span></h2>
          <p>During the open season a person may transport a protected wild animal within the state, and to a destination outside the state, if the animal may be lawfully sold and the transportation is not otherwise prohibited.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.521.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Transporting unlawfully obtained animals.</span></h2>
          <p>A person may not transport wild animals taken, bought, sold, or possessed in violation of the game and fish laws.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.521.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.75.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 75</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/259/#laws.0.23.0">1991 c 259 s 23</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.525">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.525 TRANSPORTING WILD ANIMALS BY COMMON CARRIER.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.525.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Generally.</span></h2>
          <p>A person may transport wild animals by common carrier without being in the vehicle if the person has the license required to take the animals and they are shipped to the person or to a licensed taxidermist, tanner, or fur buyer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.525.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/176/#laws.2.71.0">2009 c 176 art 2 s 71</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.525.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Employee of carrier.</span></h2>
          <p>An employee of a carrier may not transport wild animals as baggage while performing duties for the carrier.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.525.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Statement required for protected wild animals.</span></h2>
          <p>A person that transports protected wild animals by common carrier, including animals carried in baggage, must attach a statement to each shipment. The statement must include the name, address, and license number of the person shipping the animals, the number and species of the animals in the shipment, and the signature of the licensee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.525.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Shipper's license required.</span></h2>
          <p>A common carrier may not accept a shipment of big or small game unless the carrier is shown the license of the shipper to take the game.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.525.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Waybill requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>The waybill or receipt issued by a common carrier to a shipper must specify the number and species of wild animals being shipped.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.525.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Animals in possession of shipper.</span></h2>
          <p>Wild animals that are transported by common carrier are considered to be in the possession of the shipper.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.525.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.76.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 76</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/373/#laws.0.10.0">1987 c 373 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/176/#laws.2.37.0">2009 c 176 art 2 s 37</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.531">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.531 SHIPPING WILD ANIMALS TAKEN IN CANADA.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.531.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Shipping coupons.</span></h2>
          <p>A person may ship, within or out of the state, wild animals lawfully taken and possessed in Canada and that have lawfully entered the state. The shipment must have the shipping coupons required for a shipment originating in the province where the animals were taken.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.531.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/220/#laws.0.141.0">1995 c 220 s 141</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/224/#laws.0.126.0">1995 c 224 s 126</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/1/#laws.0.48.0">1Sp1995 c 1 s 48</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.531.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>

        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.531.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>

        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.531.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/220/#laws.0.141.0">1995 c 220 s 141</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/224/#laws.0.126.0">1995 c 224 s 126</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/1/#laws.0.48.0">1Sp1995 c 1 s 48</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.531.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>

        </div>

          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>

        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.531.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.77.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 77</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/269/#laws.0.8.0">1993 c 269 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/479/#laws.0.1.0">1994 c 479 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/623/#laws.1.27.0">1994 c 623 art 1 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/224/#laws.0.51.0">1995 c 224 s 51</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/1/#laws.0.25.0">1Sp1995 c 1 s 25</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.535">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.535 POSSESSING AND TRANSPORTING DEER, BEAR, ELK, AND MOOSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.535.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Validation required.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A person may not possess or transport deer, bear, elk, or moose taken in the state unless the person has the required license and validation for that animal as prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The license must be validated at the site of the kill as prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.535.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Registration required.</span></h2>
          <p>Deer, bear, elk, and moose must be registered as prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.535.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Quartering deer allowed.</span></h2>
          <p>A deer that has been validated as required in subdivision 1 may be quartered at the site of the kill. The animal's head must remain attached to one of the quarters. The quarters must be presented together for registration under subdivision 2 and must remain together until the deer is processed for storage.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.535.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Transportation period restricted.</span></h2>
          <p>A person may transport deer, bear, one elk, or moose during the open season and the two days following the season, and afterwards as prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.535.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Transporting by person other than licensee.</span></h2>
          <p>A person other than the licensee may transport deer, bear, elk, or moose that the licensee has registered as prescribed by the commissioner. The person transporting the animal must possess documentation with the address, license number, and full legal name of the licensee and the locations from which and to which the animal is being transported.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.535.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Heads, hides, and claws.</span></h2>
          <p>A resident that has a license to take deer, bear, elk, or moose may transport the head or hide of the animal within or out of the state for mounting or tanning. The hides of deer, bear, elk, and moose, and the claws of bear legally taken and with the tags that are required by this section, may be bought, sold, and transported at any time.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.535.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.78.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 78</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/149/#laws.1.36.0">1987 c 149 art 1 s 36</a>,37; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/373/#laws.0.11.0">1987 c 373 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/241/#laws.0.6.0">1991 c 241 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/231/#laws.0.32.0">1993 c 231 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/410/#laws.0.37.0">1996 c 410 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/281/#laws.2.33.0">2006 c 281 art 2 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/368/#laws.2.37.0">2008 c 368 art 2 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/90/#laws.1.37.0">2024 c 90 art 1 s 37</a>-40</p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendments to subdivisions 1, 2, 2a, and 4 by Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, sections 37 to 40, are effective upon full implementation of the replacement electronic license system. The commissioner of natural resources must notify the revisor of statutes when the replacement electronic license system is fully implemented. Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, section 52.</p>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.97A.541"><b>97A.541</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/269/#laws.0.32.0">1993 c 269 s 32</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.545">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.545 TRANSPORTING GAME BIRDS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.545.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Residents shipping by common carrier.</span></h2>
          <p>A resident that ships game birds to the resident by common carrier without being in the vehicle may not make more than three shipments during a license year. A shipment may not contain more than the resident's daily limit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.545.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Nonresidents shipping by common carrier.</span></h2>
          <p>A nonresident that ships game birds to the nonresident by common carrier without being in the vehicle must obtain a shipping permit from the commissioner. The commissioner shall issue the permit upon request, without a fee. The carrier receiving the shipment must cancel the permit as prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.545.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Shipping to other persons.</span></h2>
          <p>A person must obtain a permit from the commissioner to ship game birds to another person within or out of the state. The person must have the licenses required to take the game birds.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.545.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Game birds taken outside state.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A person may transport into the state game birds that are lawfully taken and possessed outside of this state.</p>
          <p>(b) A resident may ship the game birds by common carrier within the state. A nonresident may ship the game birds out of the state by common carrier. Each shipment must be tagged or sealed by a conservation officer as prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.545.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Undressed birds; exceptions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as provided in paragraph (b), a person may ship or otherwise transport game birds in an undressed condition only.</p>
          <p>(b) Paragraph (a) does not apply if the birds being shipped or otherwise transported:</p>
          <p>(1) were taken on a shooting preserve and are marked or identified in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.121#stat.97A.121.5">97A.121, subdivision 5</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) were taken, dressed, and lawfully shipped or otherwise transported in another state;</p>
          <p>(3) are migratory game birds that were lawfully tagged and packed by a federally permitted migratory bird preservation facility; or</p>
          <p>(4) are doves shipped or transported in accordance with federal law.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.545.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.80.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 80</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/149/#laws.1.38.0">1987 c 149 art 1 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/384/#laws.1.11.0">1987 c 384 art 1 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/231/#laws.0.33.0">1993 c 231 s 33</a>-36; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/215/#laws.0.17.0">2004 c 215 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/2/#laws.5.36.0">1Sp2011 c 2 art 5 s 36</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.551">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.551 POSSESSING AND TRANSPORTING FISH.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.551.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/149/#laws.1.54.0">1987 c 149 art 1 s 54</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.551.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Fish transported through state.</span></h2>
          <p>A person may not transport game fish or native rough fish taken in another state or country through the state during the closed season or in excess of the possession limit unless the fish are:</p>
          <p>(1) transported by common carrier; or</p>
          <p>(2) tagged, sealed, or marked as prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.551.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Shipping fish.</span></h2>
          <p>A person that has a license to take fish may make three shipments of fish in a license year to any person within or out of the state after obtaining a permit for each shipment from the commissioner. A shipment may not contain more than a possession limit of one species of fish per licensee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.551.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Walleye; northern pike.</span></h2>
          <p>Walleye and northern pike may be possessed, transported, or shipped in a dressed or undressed condition.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.551.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Preparing and packing fish for transporting.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may adopt rules for the preparation and packing of fish for transportation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.551.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote"> Registration.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may, by rule, require persons taking, possessing, and transporting certain species of fish to possess an endorsement for and register the fish. A person may not possess or transport a fish species taken in the state for which a special fish-management registration is required unless the fish is registered or validated in a manner prescribed by the commissioner. The commissioner shall prescribe the manner of issuance and the type of endorsement as authorized under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97C.087">97C.087</a>. Immediately upon reducing the fish to possession, the licensee must validate the license and the license must remain with the fish until the fish is registered. Species for which a special fish management registration is required must be transported undressed, except as otherwise prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.551.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.1.81.0">1986 c 386 art 1 s 81</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/149/#laws.1.39.0">1987 c 149 art 1 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/185/#laws.0.1.0">1993 c 185 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/231/#laws.0.37.0">1993 c 231 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.104.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 104</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/281/#laws.2.34.0">2006 c 281 art 2 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/90/#laws.1.41.0">2024 c 90 art 1 s 41</a>; art 2 s 16</p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendment to subdivision 6 by Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, section 41, is effective upon full implementation of the replacement electronic license system. The commissioner of natural resources must notify the revisor of statutes when the replacement electronic license system is fully implemented. Laws 2024, chapter 90, article 1, section 52.</p>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.97A.552"><b>97A.552</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/277/#laws.1.91.0">2012 c 277 art 1 s 91</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.97A.56">
        <h1 class="shn">97A.56 FERAL SWINE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.56.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The definitions in this subdivision apply to this section.</p>
          <p>(b) "Domestic hogs" means members of the subspecies <i>Sus scrofa domesticus</i>.</p>
          <p>(c) "Feral swine" means an animal of the family Suidae or Tayassuidae that lives in the wild or has lived in the wild during any part of the animal's lifetime. Feral swine includes released domestic hogs, unless the owner satisfies the notification requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/17.457#stat.17.457.4">17.457, subdivision 4</a>, and recovers the released domestic hogs within 72 hours of notification.</p>
          <p>(d) "Release" has the meaning given under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/17.457#stat.17.457.1">17.457, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.56.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Prohibited actions; penalty.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Unless authorized by permit under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/17.457#stat.17.457.3">17.457, subdivision 3</a>, a person may not possess or release feral swine or otherwise allow feral swine to run at large.</p>
          <p>(b) A person may not hunt or trap feral swine, except as authorized by the commissioner for feral swine control or eradication. It is not a violation of this section if a person shoots a feral swine and reports the taking to the commissioner within 24 hours. All feral swine taken in this manner must be surrendered to the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(c) A person who violates this subdivision is guilty of a misdemeanor.</p>
          <p>(d) A person who violates this subdivision is liable for the actual costs incurred by the state for the possession or release of the feral swine.</p>
          <p>(e) A person who violates this subdivision is liable for the damages caused by the possession or release of the feral swine.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.56.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Authorized removal of feral swine.</span></h2>
          <p>A person authorized under game and fish laws to take feral swine is not liable to the owner for the value of the animals.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.97A.56.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Domestic hogs and feral swine response protocols.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner, in cooperation with the commissioner of agriculture and the Board of Animal Health, must develop protocols for responding to the release of domestic hogs and feral swine, including reporting requirements, interagency communications, and other actions necessary to resolve the release.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.97A.56.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/1/4/#laws.5.18.0">1Sp2015 c 4 art 5 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/90/#laws.4.5.0">2024 c 90 art 4 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/116/#laws.6.4.0">2024 c 116 art 6 s 4</a>,5</p>
      </div>
    </div>
